fly-wbe · the parameter record

Champion Parameters

Every champion is the same measured wiring with a set of numbers on top, the theta. This page shows, for the current champion, what those numbers are and how they reach each of the 11,5xx cell types, including the cells that get their own rules, and it keeps every champion's theta of record with its hash and git tag so the exact recipe of any past fly can always be recovered.

A theta holds shared scalars (one number for every neuron), modes (a rule that turns measured structure into a per-neuron value, such as delay from cable length by cell type), and a few named maps. Most cell types therefore share one row of numbers and differ only in the values the rules derive. The table lists what the simulation holds after the rules are applied, read from the engine's own network build.

Current champion: wm1

188,508 neurons in 11,533 cell-type groups (untyped cells grouped by class). Per-type table: champion-params/wm1.csv in the repository.
